#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

The factorization, matrix generation, and validation all contain cubic-time loops. I’ll move those computations to CUDA, using a blocked factorization and tiled matrix kernels. The random sequence, double precision, command-line options, and result format will stay the same.

/bin/bash -lc "g++ -O3 -march=native -std=c++20 -x c++ - -o /tmp/cholesky-original <<'CHOLESKY_ORIGINAL'
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L"'^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu'"\\n\", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B"'^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B''^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L''^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L''^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e'"\\n\", maxError);
    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {

        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");
    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");

    std::vector<double> A_orig;

    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;

    if (printResults) {

        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");

        if (valid) {
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {

            return 1;
        }
    }

+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with sixteen double accumulators per
+// thread. The two row-major operands are staged in padded shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are scheduled; generation also writes the symmetric half.
+enum GramOperation { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+template <GramOperation Operation>
+__global__ void gramKernel(const double* input, double* output,
+                           const double* original, double* errors,

+// Solve L_panel * L_diagonal^T = A_panel. Rows are independent; cooperative
+// loads/stores make the row-major global-memory transfers coalesced.
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first,
+                           int width, const int* info) {
+    if (*info != 0) return;
+    __shared__ double diagonal[Panel][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double rows[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + width + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (int index = tid; index < Panel * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        diagonal[row][col] = row < width && col <= row
+            ? matrix[(first + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    for (int index = tid; index < Tile * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        rows[row][col] = rowBase + row < n && col < width
+            ? matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+

+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int col = 0; col < Panel; ++col) {
+            if (col < width) {
+                double sum = 0.0;
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int k = 0; k < col; ++k) {

+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        if (rowBase + row < n && col < width) {
+            matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] = rows[row][col];
+        }
+    }
+}

+__global__ void zeroUpper(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    const size_t count = n * n;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        if (index % n > index / n) matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(double* matrix, size_t n, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    // Preserve the original rand_r sequence exactly, rather than substituting
+    // a GPU random generator with a different matrix for the same seed.
+    std::vector<double> random(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : random) {
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / double(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceRandom(random.size());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceRandom.get(), random.data(),
+                               random.size() * sizeof(double),
+                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    gramKernel<Generate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+        deviceRandom.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n, nullptr);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The random operand and its host storage must outlive the kernel.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(double* matrix, size_t n, int* info, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(info, 0, sizeof(int), stream));
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += Panel) {
+        const int width = int(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - first));
+        factorDiagonal<<<1, Panel, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        const size_t next = first + width;
+        if (next < n) {
+            const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n - next + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+            solvePanel<<<tiles, Tile, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            gramKernel<Update><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, next, first, next, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    if (n) {
+        const unsigned blocks = unsigned(std::min(size_t(65535), (n * n + 255) / 256));
+        zeroUpper<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+    int hostInfo = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&hostInfo, info, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    if (hostInfo) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+               size_t(hostInfo - 1));
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}

         printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");

     }

+// Retain the original host-vector interfaces; these also always execute on CUDA.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<int> info(1);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const bool success = choleskyDecomposition(matrix.get(), n, info.get(), stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    return success;
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(matrix.get(), n, stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig,
+                      const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<double> original(n * n);
+    if (n) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), L.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(original.get(), A_orig.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    }

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {
+    if (result != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));
+    }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

+class CudaStream {
+public:
+    CudaStream() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream_); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    operator cudaStream_t() const { return stream_; }
+private:
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Matrix is too large");
+        }
+        if (count) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Threads = 16;
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with sixteen double accumulators per
+// thread. The two row-major operands are staged in padded shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are scheduled; generation also writes the symmetric half.
+enum GramOperation { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+template <GramOperation Operation>
+__global__ void gramKernel(const double* input, double* output,
+                           const double* original, double* errors,
+                           size_t n, size_t first, size_t begin, size_t end,
+                           const int* info) {
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) return;
+    if (Operation == Update && *info != 0) return;
+
+    __shared__ double left[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double maxima[2][8];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t colBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    // L is triangular: no terms beyond the last column of this tile can
+    // contribute to L * L^T.
+    if (Operation == Validate) end = min(end, colBase + Tile);

+// Solve L_panel * L_diagonal^T = A_panel. Rows are independent; cooperative
+// loads/stores make the row-major global-memory transfers coalesced.
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first,
+                           int width, const int* info) {
+    if (*info != 0) return;
+    __shared__ double diagonal[Panel][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double rows[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + width + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (int index = tid; index < Panel * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        diagonal[row][col] = row < width && col <= row
+            ? matrix[(first + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    for (int index = tid; index < Tile * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        rows[row][col] = rowBase + row < n && col < width
+            ? matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+

+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int col = 0; col < Panel; ++col) {
+            if (col < width) {
+                double sum = 0.0;
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int k = 0; k < col; ++k) {

+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        if (rowBase + row < n && col < width) {
+            matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] = rows[row][col];

+__global__ void zeroUpper(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    const size_t count = n * n;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        if (index % n > index / n) matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(double* matrix, size_t n, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    // Preserve the original rand_r sequence exactly, rather than substituting
+    // a GPU random generator with a different matrix for the same seed.
+    std::vector<double> random(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : random) {
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / double(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceRandom(random.size());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceRandom.get(), random.data(),
+                               random.size() * sizeof(double),
+                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    gramKernel<Generate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+        deviceRandom.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n, nullptr);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The random operand and its host storage must outlive the kernel.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(double* matrix, size_t n, int* info, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(info, 0, sizeof(int), stream));
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += Panel) {
+        const int width = int(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - first));
+        factorDiagonal<<<1, Panel, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        const size_t next = first + width;
+        if (next < n) {
+            const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n - next + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+            solvePanel<<<tiles, Tile, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            gramKernel<Update><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, next, first, next, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    if (n) {
+        const unsigned blocks = unsigned(std::min(size_t(65535), (n * n + 255) / 256));
+        zeroUpper<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+    int hostInfo = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&hostInfo, info, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    if (hostInfo) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+               size_t(hostInfo - 1));
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}

The CUDA implementation is in place. It keeps the matrix on the GPU through generation, factorization, and validation, and uses shared memory for tiled updates. I’m checking the build, results against the CPU version, and behavior at tile boundaries.

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {
+    if (result != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));
+    }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

+class CudaStream {
+public:
+    CudaStream() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream_); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    operator cudaStream_t() const { return stream_; }
+private:
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Matrix is too large");
+        }
+        if (count) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Threads = 16;
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with sixteen double accumulators per
+// thread. The two row-major operands are staged in padded shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are scheduled; generation also writes the symmetric half.
+enum GramOperation { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+template <GramOperation Operation>
+__global__ void gramKernel(const double* input, double* output,
+                           const double* original, double* errors,
+                           size_t n, size_t first, size_t begin, size_t end,
+                           const int* info) {
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) return;
+    if (Operation == Update && *info != 0) return;
+
+    __shared__ double left[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double maxima[2][8];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t colBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    // L is triangular: no terms beyond the last column of this tile can
+    // contribute to L * L^T.
+    if (Operation == Validate) end = min(end, colBase + Tile);

+            const int row = index / Panel;
+            const int k = index % Panel;
+            left[row][k] = rowBase + row < n && k < width
+                ? input[(rowBase + row) * n + base + k] : 0.0;
+            right[row][k] = colBase + row < n && k < width
+                ? input[(colBase + row) * n + base + k] : 0.0;

+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (row < n && col < n && row >= col) {
+                const size_t index = row * n + col;
+                if (Operation == Generate) {
+                    const double value = sums[i][j] + (row == col ? double(n) : 0.0);
+                    output[index] = value;
+                    if (row != col) output[col * n + row] = value;
+                } else if (Operation == Update) {
+                    output[index] -= sums[i][j];
+                } else {

+        const int warp = tid / 32;
+        for (int offset = 16; offset; offset /= 2) {
+            maxAbsolute = fmax(maxAbsolute, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, maxAbsolute, offset));
+            maxRelative = fmax(maxRelative, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, maxRelative, offset));
+        }

+            maxima[1][warp] = maxRelative;

+// Solve L_panel * L_diagonal^T = A_panel. Rows are independent; cooperative
+// loads/stores make the row-major global-memory transfers coalesced.
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first,
+                           int width, const int* info) {
+    if (*info != 0) return;
+    __shared__ double diagonal[Panel][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double rows[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + width + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (int index = tid; index < Panel * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        diagonal[row][col] = row < width && col <= row
+            ? matrix[(first + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    for (int index = tid; index < Tile * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        rows[row][col] = rowBase + row < n && col < width
+            ? matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (rowBase + tid < n) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int col = 0; col < Panel; ++col) {
+            if (col < width) {
+                double sum = 0.0;
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                    sum = fma(rows[tid][k], diagonal[col][k], sum);
+                }
+                rows[tid][col] = (rows[tid][col] - sum) / diagonal[col][col];

-    
-    // Compare with original
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int index = tid; index < Tile * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        if (rowBase + row < n && col < width) {
+            matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] = rows[row][col];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpper(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    const size_t count = n * n;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        if (index % n > index / n) matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(double* matrix, size_t n, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    // Preserve the original rand_r sequence exactly, rather than substituting
+    // a GPU random generator with a different matrix for the same seed.
+    std::vector<double> random(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : random) {
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / double(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceRandom(random.size());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceRandom.get(), random.data(),
+                               random.size() * sizeof(double),
+                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    gramKernel<Generate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+        deviceRandom.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n, nullptr);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The random operand and its host storage must outlive the kernel.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(double* matrix, size_t n, int* info, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(info, 0, sizeof(int), stream));
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += Panel) {
+        const int width = int(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - first));
+        factorDiagonal<<<1, Panel, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        const size_t next = first + width;
+        if (next < n) {
+            const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n - next + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+            solvePanel<<<tiles, Tile, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            gramKernel<Update><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, next, first, next, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    if (n) {
+        const unsigned blocks = unsigned(std::min(size_t(65535), (n * n + 255) / 256));
+        zeroUpper<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+    int hostInfo = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&hostInfo, info, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    if (hostInfo) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+               size_t(hostInfo - 1));
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}

+// Retain the original host-vector interfaces; these also always execute on CUDA.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<int> info(1);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const bool success = choleskyDecomposition(matrix.get(), n, info.get(), stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    return success;
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(matrix.get(), n, stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}

+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig,
+                      const size_t n) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {
+    if (result != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));
+    }
+}

+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

+class CudaStream {
+public:
+    CudaStream() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream_); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    operator cudaStream_t() const { return stream_; }
+private:
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Matrix is too large");
+        }
+        if (count) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Threads = 16;
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with sixteen double accumulators per
+// thread. The two row-major operands are staged in padded shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are scheduled; generation also writes the symmetric half.
+enum GramOperation { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+template <GramOperation Operation>
+__global__ void gramKernel(const double* input, double* output,
+                           const double* original, double* errors,
+                           size_t n, size_t first, size_t begin, size_t end,
+                           const int* info) {
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) return;
+    if (Operation == Update && *info != 0) return;
+
+    __shared__ double left[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double maxima[2][8];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t colBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    // L is triangular: no terms beyond the last column of this tile can
+    // contribute to L * L^T.
+    if (Operation == Validate) end = min(end, colBase + Tile);

+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * Threads;

+                    const double value = sums[i][j] + (row == col ? double(n) : 0.0);
+                    output[index] = value;
+                    if (row != col) output[col * n + row] = value;

-bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {

+// Solve L_panel * L_diagonal^T = A_panel. Rows are independent; cooperative
+// loads/stores make the row-major global-memory transfers coalesced.
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first,
+                           int width, const int* info) {
+    if (*info != 0) return;
+    __shared__ double diagonal[Panel][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double rows[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + width + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (int index = tid; index < Panel * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        diagonal[row][col] = row < width && col <= row
+            ? matrix[(first + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    for (int index = tid; index < Tile * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        rows[row][col] = rowBase + row < n && col < width
+            ? matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+

+        #pragma unroll

+                double sum = 0.0;
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int k = 0; k < col; ++k) {

+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        if (rowBase + row < n && col < width) {
+            matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] = rows[row][col];
+        }
+    }

+
+__global__ void zeroUpper(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    const size_t count = n * n;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        if (index % n > index / n) matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(double* matrix, size_t n, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    // Preserve the original rand_r sequence exactly, rather than substituting
+    // a GPU random generator with a different matrix for the same seed.
+    std::vector<double> random(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : random) {
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / double(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceRandom(random.size());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceRandom.get(), random.data(),
+                               random.size() * sizeof(double),
+                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    gramKernel<Generate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+        deviceRandom.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n, nullptr);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The random operand and its host storage must outlive the kernel.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(double* matrix, size_t n, int* info, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(info, 0, sizeof(int), stream));
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += Panel) {
+        const int width = int(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - first));
+        factorDiagonal<<<1, Panel, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        const size_t next = first + width;
+        if (next < n) {
+            const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n - next + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+            solvePanel<<<tiles, Tile, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            gramKernel<Update><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, next, first, next, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    if (n) {
+        const unsigned blocks = unsigned(std::min(size_t(65535), (n * n + 255) / 256));
+        zeroUpper<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+    int hostInfo = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&hostInfo, info, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    if (hostInfo) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+               size_t(hostInfo - 1));
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+

-        relError = std::max(relError, rel);

+        std::vector<double> errors(2 * size_t(tiles) * tiles);
+        DeviceBuffer<double> deviceErrors(errors.size());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(deviceErrors.get(), 0, errors.size() * sizeof(double), stream));
+        gramKernel<Validate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(

+                                   errors.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));

         printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
         return false;
     }

+} // namespace
+
+// Retain the original host-vector interfaces; these also always execute on CUDA.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<int> info(1);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const bool success = choleskyDecomposition(matrix.get(), n, info.get(), stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    return success;
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(matrix.get(), n, stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig,
+                      const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<double> original(n * n);
+    if (n) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), L.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(original.get(), A_orig.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    }
+    return validateCholesky(matrix.get(), original.get(), n, stream);
+}

+class CudaStream {
+public:

+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream_); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    operator cudaStream_t() const { return stream_; }
+private:

+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Matrix is too large");
+        }
+        if (count) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Threads = 16;
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with sixteen double accumulators per
+// thread. The two row-major operands are staged in padded shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are scheduled; generation also writes the symmetric half.
+enum GramOperation { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+template <GramOperation Operation>
+__global__ void gramKernel(const double* input, double* output,
+                           const double* original, double* errors,
+                           size_t n, size_t first, size_t begin, size_t end,
+                           const int* info) {
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) return;
+    if (Operation == Update && *info != 0) return;
+
+    __shared__ double left[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double maxima[2][8];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t colBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    // L is triangular: no terms beyond the last column of this tile can
+    // contribute to L * L^T.
+    if (Operation == Validate) end = min(end, colBase + Tile);

+// Solve L_panel * L_diagonal^T = A_panel. Rows are independent; cooperative
+// loads/stores make the row-major global-memory transfers coalesced.
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first,
+                           int width, const int* info) {
+    if (*info != 0) return;
+    __shared__ double diagonal[Panel][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double rows[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + width + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (int index = tid; index < Panel * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        diagonal[row][col] = row < width && col <= row
+            ? matrix[(first + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }

+        if (rowBase + row < n && col < width) {
+            matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] = rows[row][col];
+        }

+__global__ void zeroUpper(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    const size_t count = n * n;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        if (index % n > index / n) matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(double* matrix, size_t n, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    // Preserve the original rand_r sequence exactly, rather than substituting
+    // a GPU random generator with a different matrix for the same seed.
+    std::vector<double> random(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : random) {
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / double(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceRandom(random.size());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceRandom.get(), random.data(),
+                               random.size() * sizeof(double),
+                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    gramKernel<Generate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+        deviceRandom.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n, nullptr);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The random operand and its host storage must outlive the kernel.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(double* matrix, size_t n, int* info, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(info, 0, sizeof(int), stream));
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += Panel) {
+        const int width = int(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - first));
+        factorDiagonal<<<1, Panel, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        const size_t next = first + width;
+        if (next < n) {
+            const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n - next + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+            solvePanel<<<tiles, Tile, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            gramKernel<Update><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, next, first, next, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    if (n) {
+        const unsigned blocks = unsigned(std::min(size_t(65535), (n * n + 255) / 256));
+        zeroUpper<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+    int hostInfo = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&hostInfo, info, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    if (hostInfo) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+               size_t(hostInfo - 1));
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+

-        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
-        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
+    if (n) {
+        const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+        std::vector<double> errors(2 * size_t(tiles) * tiles);
+        DeviceBuffer<double> deviceErrors(errors.size());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(deviceErrors.get(), 0, errors.size() * sizeof(double), stream));
+        gramKernel<Validate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+            matrix, nullptr, original, deviceErrors.get(), n, 0, 0, n, nullptr);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+                                   errors.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < errors.size(); i += 2) {

     }

     if (relError > 1e-6) {
         printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
         return false;
     }

+} // namespace
+
+// Retain the original host-vector interfaces; these also always execute on CUDA.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<int> info(1);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const bool success = choleskyDecomposition(matrix.get(), n, info.get(), stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    return success;
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(matrix.get(), n, stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig,
+                      const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<double> original(n * n);
+    if (n) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), L.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(original.get(), A_orig.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    }
+    return validateCholesky(matrix.get(), original.get(), n, stream);
+}

+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));

+class CudaStream {
+public:
+    CudaStream() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream_); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    operator cudaStream_t() const { return stream_; }
+private:
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Matrix is too large");
+        }
+        if (count) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Threads = 16;
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with sixteen double accumulators per
+// thread. The two row-major operands are staged in padded shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are scheduled; generation also writes the symmetric half.
+enum GramOperation { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+template <GramOperation Operation>
+__global__ void gramKernel(const double* input, double* output,
+                           const double* original, double* errors,
+                           size_t n, size_t first, size_t begin, size_t end,
+                           const int* info) {
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) return;
+    if (Operation == Update && *info != 0) return;
+
+    __shared__ double left[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double maxima[2][8];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t colBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    // L is triangular: no terms beyond the last column of this tile can
+    // contribute to L * L^T.
+    if (Operation == Validate) end = min(end, colBase + Tile);

-bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {

+// Solve L_panel * L_diagonal^T = A_panel. Rows are independent; cooperative
+// loads/stores make the row-major global-memory transfers coalesced.
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first,
+                           int width, const int* info) {
+    if (*info != 0) return;
+    __shared__ double diagonal[Panel][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double rows[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + width + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (int index = tid; index < Panel * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        diagonal[row][col] = row < width && col <= row
+            ? matrix[(first + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }

+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        rows[row][col] = rowBase + row < n && col < width
+            ? matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (rowBase + tid < n) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int col = 0; col < Panel; ++col) {
+            if (col < width) {
+                double sum = 0.0;
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                    sum = fma(rows[tid][k], diagonal[col][k], sum);

+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        if (rowBase + row < n && col < width) {
+            matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] = rows[row][col];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpper(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    const size_t count = n * n;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        if (index % n > index / n) matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(double* matrix, size_t n, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    // Preserve the original rand_r sequence exactly, rather than substituting
+    // a GPU random generator with a different matrix for the same seed.
+    std::vector<double> random(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : random) {
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / double(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceRandom(random.size());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceRandom.get(), random.data(),
+                               random.size() * sizeof(double),
+                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    gramKernel<Generate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+        deviceRandom.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n, nullptr);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The random operand and its host storage must outlive the kernel.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(double* matrix, size_t n, int* info, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(info, 0, sizeof(int), stream));
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += Panel) {
+        const int width = int(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - first));
+        factorDiagonal<<<1, Panel, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        const size_t next = first + width;
+        if (next < n) {
+            const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n - next + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+            solvePanel<<<tiles, Tile, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            gramKernel<Update><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, next, first, next, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    if (n) {

+// Retain the original host-vector interfaces; these also always execute on CUDA.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<int> info(1);

+                                     cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));

+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    return success;

+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));

+class CudaStream {
+public:
+    CudaStream() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream_); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    operator cudaStream_t() const { return stream_; }
+private:
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;

+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Matrix is too large");
+        }
+        if (count) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Threads = 16;
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with sixteen double accumulators per
+// thread. The two row-major operands are staged in padded shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are scheduled; generation also writes the symmetric half.
+enum GramOperation { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+template <GramOperation Operation>
+__global__ void gramKernel(const double* input, double* output,
+                           const double* original, double* errors,
+                           size_t n, size_t first, size_t begin, size_t end,
+                           const int* info) {
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) return;
+    if (Operation == Update && *info != 0) return;
+
+    __shared__ double left[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double maxima[2][8];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t colBase = first + size_t(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    // L is triangular: no terms beyond the last column of this tile can
+    // contribute to L * L^T.
+    if (Operation == Validate) end = min(end, colBase + Tile);

+// loads/stores make the row-major global-memory transfers coalesced.
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first,
+                           int width, const int* info) {

+    for (int index = tid; index < Panel * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        diagonal[row][col] = row < width && col <= row
+            ? matrix[(first + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    for (int index = tid; index < Tile * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        rows[row][col] = rowBase + row < n && col < width
+            ? matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (rowBase + tid < n) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int col = 0; col < Panel; ++col) {
+            if (col < width) {
+                double sum = 0.0;
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                    sum = fma(rows[tid][k], diagonal[col][k], sum);
+                }
+                rows[tid][col] = (rows[tid][col] - sum) / diagonal[col][col];
             }
-            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
         }
     }
-    
-    // Compare with original
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int index = tid; index < Tile * Panel; index += Tile) {
+        const int row = index / Panel;
+        const int col = index % Panel;
+        if (rowBase + row < n && col < width) {
+            matrix[(rowBase + row) * n + first + col] = rows[row][col];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpper(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    const size_t count = n * n;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        if (index % n > index / n) matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(double* matrix, size_t n, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    // Preserve the original rand_r sequence exactly, rather than substituting
+    // a GPU random generator with a different matrix for the same seed.
+    std::vector<double> random(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : random) {
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / double(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceRandom(random.size());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceRandom.get(), random.data(),
+                               random.size() * sizeof(double),
+                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    gramKernel<Generate><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+        deviceRandom.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n, nullptr);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The random operand and its host storage must outlive the kernel.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(double* matrix, size_t n, int* info, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(info, 0, sizeof(int), stream));
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += Panel) {
+        const int width = int(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - first));
+        factorDiagonal<<<1, Panel, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        const size_t next = first + width;
+        if (next < n) {
+            const unsigned tiles = unsigned((n - next + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+            solvePanel<<<tiles, Tile, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n, first, width, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            gramKernel<Update><<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(Threads, Threads), 0, stream>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, next, first, next, info);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    if (n) {
+        const unsigned blocks = unsigned(std::min(size_t(65535), (n * n + 255) / 256));
+        zeroUpper<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(matrix, n);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+    int hostInfo = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&hostInfo, info, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    if (hostInfo) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+               size_t(hostInfo - 1));
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}

+        DeviceBuffer<double> deviceErrors(errors.size());

+                                   errors.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));

         printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
         return false;
     }

     return true;

+// Retain the original host-vector interfaces; these also always execute on CUDA.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<int> info(1);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    const bool success = choleskyDecomposition(matrix.get(), n, info.get(), stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+    return success;
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(matrix.get(), n, stream);
+    if (n) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), matrix.get(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig,
+                      const size_t n) {
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> matrix(n * n);
+    DeviceBuffer<double> original(n * n);
+    if (n) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(matrix.get(), L.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(original.get(), A_orig.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+    }
+    return validateCholesky(matrix.get(), original.get(), n, stream);
+}

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {
+    if (result != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));

+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

+class CudaStream {
+public:
+    CudaStream() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream_); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    operator cudaStream_t() const { return stream_; }
+private:
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("Matrix is too large");
+        }
+        if (count) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
